### [102] [Lightweight CNN-Based Anomaly Detection for High Voltage Converter Modulators in the Spallation Neutron Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31259)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Julián Luengo,Isaac Triguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高功率脉冲变换器故障检测中，架构归纳偏置（时间滤波与跨通道混合的顺序）对多通道信号处理性能的影响。通过在SNS的HVCM数据集上实验，最佳变体（结合自适应通道重加权）在AUC-PR=0.816和AUC-ROC=0.934上超越了现有最优方法，并揭示了不同故障类型对单通道幅度变化或多通道联合表示的依赖差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率脉冲变换器的非计划停机是大型加速器设施停机的主要来源，而现有深度学习方法在早期卷积层就混合了时间与跨通道操作，缺乏显式建模通道独立性与结构化交互的机制，作者假设架构归纳偏置（即时间滤波与跨通道混合的顺序）对此类数据的检测性能至关重要。

Method: 在HVCM多通道脉冲数据上，改变时间滤波和跨通道混合操作的执行顺序，引入每脉冲自适应通道重加权机制，并在SNS四个子系统（RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL）以及六类故障家族上评估多种架构变体。

Result: 最佳变体在池化指标上达到AUC-PR=0.816和AUC-ROC=0.934，在大多数子系统和五类故障家族上超越现有最优方法。消融实验识别出三个主导输入通道，并将不同故障家族的性能差异归因于其前兆表现为单通道幅度偏移还是需要联合通道表示才能发现的细微模式。

Conclusion: 时间滤波与跨通道混合的先后顺序（架构归纳偏置）显著影响故障检测性能，自适应通道重加权能进一步提升敏感度，不同故障类型对单通道vs联合通道表示有不同偏好。

Abstract: Unscheduled trips of high-power pulsed converters are a leading source of downtime at large accelerator facilities. At the Spallation Neutron Source (SNS), the High Voltage Converter Modulators (HVCMs) are consistently the second-largest contributor to lost beam time. Each HVCM pulse is recorded across sensor channels spanning currents, voltages, and magnetic fluxes, whose mutual interactions encode the operating state of the system. Fault precursors do not manifest uniformly across these channels: depending on fault type, they may alter the temporal structure of individual signals, change the statistical dependencies among channels, or both. Existing deep-learning approaches typically process multi-channel signals with standard convolutional pipelines that entangle temporal and cross-channel operations from the first layer, giving the model no explicit mechanism to represent channel independence or structured inter-channel interaction. We hypothesise that architectural inductive bias, specifically the ordering of temporal filtering and cross-channel mixing, plays a central role in detection performance on this class of data. To test this, we vary the order in which these two operations are applied, and examine whether per-pulse adaptive channel reweighting further improves sensitivity. Evaluated on the public HVCM dataset across all four SNS subsystems (RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL), our best variant achieves a pooled AUC-PR of 0.816 and AUC-ROC of 0.934, outperforming the state of the art on most subsystems and five of the six fault families. Ablations identify three dominant input channels and link per-fault-family performance to whether precursors manifest as amplitude shifts in individual channels or as subtler patterns requiring joint channel representations to surface.

</details>


### [103] [Algorithmic Recourse of In-Context Learning for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31272)
*Wenshuo Dong,Jiaming Zhang,Shaopneg Fu,Hongbin Lin,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对上下文学习下的表格数据算法反事实解释进行研究，提出无需梯度的ASR-ICL框架以高效生成可操作且稀疏的反事实解释，理论证明了反事实的可定义性和有界性，实验验证了方法与理论收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型越来越多地部署于信用审批等高风险场景，需要为受影响的个体提供事后解释与反事实解释。虽然上下文学习已使大语言模型能直接对表格数据进行预测，但针对ICL的反事实解释研究尚属空白。

Method: 首先进行理论分析，证明反事实在ICL设定下可定义且有界，并刻画其随上下文增大向经典解收敛的行为。提出ASR-ICL框架，一种基于零阶优化的自适应子空间反事实方法，高效生成可操作且稀疏的反事实解释，并自然扩展至多分类任务。

Result: 在多个真实数据集和模型上的实验表明，ASR-ICL能以更少的查询达到与现有方法相当的反事实质量，并实证确认了预测的收敛行为，验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: 本文填补了ICL下表格数据反事实解释研究的空白，从理论和实践两方面证明了反事实解释在ICL场景中的可行性，提出的ASR-ICL框架兼具效率与质量，为高风险决策中的可解释AI提供了新工具。

Abstract: As predictive models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as credit approval, there is a growing need for post-hoc methods that provide recourse to affected individuals. Many such models operate on tabular data, where features correspond to real-world attributes. Recently, in-context learning (ICL) has enabled large language models to perform tabular prediction by conditioning on labeled examples at inference time, without explicit training. However, algorithmic recourse for tabular decision-making under ICL remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first study of algorithmic recourse for tabular data under ICL. We carry out a theoretical analysis, showing that recourse remains well-defined and bounded, and we characterize how recourse converges toward classical solutions as the context size increases. In practice, we propose a novel zeroth-order recourse framework, Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning (ASR-ICL), that efficiently generates actionable and sparse recourse for black-box ICL models. The proposed framework naturally extends to multi-class tabular tasks. Experiments across multiple real-world datasets and models demonstrate that ASR-ICL achieves recourse quality comparable to existing methods with fewer queries and empirically confirm the predicted convergence behavior, supporting our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [104] [Survival Reinforcement Learning: Toward Scalable Self-Supervised RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31273)
*Franki Nguimatsia-Tiofack,Fabian Schramm,Théotime Le Hellard,Justin Carpentier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生存强化学习(SRL)，一种基于在线分类的替代方法，通过最大化智能体在目标状态停留时间来扩展生存价值学习框架，在长时域目标条件规划任务上比对比强化学习(CRL)提升2-8倍性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我监督对比强化学习(CRL)虽具有深度缩放能力，但在长时域目标条件规划中由于对比损失固有的均匀性-容忍性困境而表现不佳。

Method: 提出生存强化学习(SRL)，这是一种基于在线分类的方法，通过最大化智能体在目标状态的停留时间来扩展生存价值学习框架，规避了CRL的结构性约束，并缓解了生存框架中固有的"bang-bang"控制问题。

Result: 在多种机器人基准任务上，缩放后的SRL在操作任务上与最先进的CRL性能相当，在稳定的长时域运动任务上性能领先2倍到8倍。

Conclusion: 基于分类的方法可能成为扩展强化学习的关键基础构建块。

Abstract: While self-supervised Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has shown remarkable depth-scaling capabilities, successfully using networks over 64 layers, scaled CRL still struggles with long-horizon goal-conditioned planning due to the uniformity-tolerance dilemma inherent in contrastive losses. We introduce Survival Reinforcement Learning (SRL), an online classification-based alternative that extends the survival value learning framework by maximizing the agent's dwell time at target goals. SRL bypasses the structural constraints of CRL and mitigates the "bang-bang" control solutions inherent to survival frameworks, which often induce undesirable behavior in complex dynamical systems. Evaluated across diverse robotic benchmarks, scaled SRL matches state-of-the-art CRL on manipulation tasks and outperforms it by 2x to 8x on stable, long-horizon locomotion tasks. Our results provide strong additional evidence that classification-based methods may serve as a key primitive in the broader effort to scale reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [105] [Learning Parametric Nitrogen Fertilizer Response Curves Using Neuro Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31276)
*Giorgio Morales,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号回归方法，无需预设函数形式即可学习作物对氮肥反应的参数化曲线，在合成数据和真实冬小麦数据上均表现出比传统模型更低的拟合误差，并能捕获不同管理区域的功能行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业中准确建模作物对氮肥的反应至关重要，但现有方法要么依赖预定义参数形式，要么使用不透明的机器学习模型，限制了从数据中解释或发现特定地点的功能关系。

Method: 提出一种神经符号回归方法，集成基于Transformer的多集合符号骨架预测策略，通过构建多样化输入子集并强制一致性约束来发现共享的功能结构，再使用遗传算法拟合观测数据。

Result: 在合成一维问题上，该方法即使在数据稀缺情况下也能恢复正确表达式；在真实冬小麦数据上，发现的表达式比二次平顶和指数函数等传统模型拟合误差更低，并捕获了不同空间区域的多样化功能行为。

Conclusion: 神经符号回归方法能够发现特定地点的农学功能关系，支持精准农业中的知情决策，具有广阔的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurately modeling crop response to Nitrogen (N) fertilization is a fundamental challenge in precision agriculture, as it impacts both economic returns and environmental sustainability. Existing approaches either rely on predefined parametric forms or opaque machine learning models, limiting their ability to interpret or discover site-specific functional relationships from data. In this work, we propose a neuro symbolic regression (SR) approach to learn parametric N-response curves without assuming a predefined functional form. Our approach integrates a transformer-based Multi-Set Symbolic Skeleton Prediction strategy, enabling the discovery of shared functional structures across multiple subdomains or management zones (MZs). By constructing diverse input subsets and enforcing consistency across them, the method recovers robust symbolic skeletons that are subsequently fitted to observed data using a genetic algorithm. This framework was first evaluated on synthetic one-dimensional problems to assess its robustness under varying levels of epistemic uncertainty. The results demonstrate the ability of the proposed SR approach to recover correct expressions even in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we present the results of applying our method to real-world winter wheat data, learning distinct parametric N-response curves for different MZs within a field. The results show that the discovered expressions not only achieve lower fitting errors than traditional models such as quadratic-plateau and exponential functions, but also capture diverse functional behaviors across spatial regions. This demonstrates the potential that neuro SR has to enable the discovery of site-specific agronomic relationships and support informed decision-making in precision agriculture.

</details>


### [106] [Interpretability Without Tradeoffs: Disentangling Polysemanticity At Equal Predictive Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31304)
*Doğukan Bağcı,Bernt Schiele,Simone Schaub-Meyer,Jonas Fischer,Robin Hesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELUDe是一种无需训练、无监督的方法，通过无损重组层间信息流，将DNN的潜在表征分解为可解释的单义子单元，在保持模型输出完全相同的前提下提升可解释性，并在多个视觉模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN的单个神经元往往编码多个不相关概念，导致模型决策过程难以理解。现有方法（如稀疏自编码器）虽能分离混合信号，但通常需要改变模型结构且会损害下游性能。

Method: ELUDe通过重新组织层间信息流动，将概念特定的贡献重新路由到清晰、可检查的子单元中，通过构造保证原始计算完全不变。无需显式训练、无需标签，可直接应用于预训练模型。

Result: 在DINOv2和监督ViT-B/16等多个视觉模型上，ELUDe提升了可解释性，下游准确率保持不变，运行高效，并支持模型表征操控等实际应用。

Conclusion: ELUDe实现了可解释性（几乎）无折衷：在不损失性能的前提下，提供更清晰、可扩展且可操作的模型洞察。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are widely used, but interpreting what they actually learn remains difficult. A major obstacle is that individual neurons often encode multiple unrelated concepts, obscuring the decision process of the network. While prior work, such as sparse autoencoders, can separate these mixed signals into more meaningful, "monosemantic" features, this typically requires altering the model in ways that can degrade downstream performance. To overcome this, we introduce ELUDe (explicit, lossless, unsupervised disentanglement), a method for improving the interpretability of DNNs while preserving their functional equivalence. ELUDe breaks latent representations into clear, inspectable sub-units that behave like interpretable features, while guaranteeing that the model's outputs remain exactly the same. It requires no explicit training, no labels, and can be applied to pretrained models. ELUDe works by reorganizing how information flows between layers, re-routing concept-specific contributions while preserving the original computation by construction. Across several vision models, including DINOv2 and supervised ViT-B/16, ELUDe improves interpretability, keeps downstream accuracy unchanged, runs efficiently, and supports practical uses such as steering model representations. In short, ELUDe offers interpretability (almost) without a tradeoff: clearer, scalable, and actionable model insights with no loss in performance.

</details>


### [107] [Non-Asymptotic Convergence of Stochastic Iterative Algorithms: A Lyapunov Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31309)
*Zaiwei Chen,Siva Theja Maguluri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于Lyapunov的随机迭代算法（随机逼近算法）有限时间分析方法，介绍了广义Moreau包络作为通用Lyapunov函数，给出了均方收敛保证，并讨论了在随机梯度下降、线性SA和强化学习算法（如Q学习、时序差分学习）中的应用，以及向马尔可夫噪声、半范数压缩算子、耗散算子和高概率界的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为随机逼近算法的有限时间分析提供一个统一且自洽的框架，特别是通过Lyapunov方法系统化地处理各类随机迭代算法的收敛性分析问题。

Method: 以广义Moreau包络作为通用Lyapunov函数，建立统一的有限时间分析框架；分别处理i.i.d.噪声和马尔可夫噪声设置，覆盖压缩算子、半范数压缩算子和耗散算子等多种算子类型。

Result: 该框架能够为随机梯度下降、线性SA、Q学习和时序差分学习等算法提供均方收敛保证，并能推广到马尔可夫噪声场景下获得高概率界。

Conclusion: Lyapunov方法为随机逼近算法的有限时间分析提供了统一且强大的工具，尤其在强化学习领域具有广泛应用前景，文中还指出了若干开放问题待进一步研究。

Abstract: We survey Lyapunov-based techniques for the finite-time analysis of stochastic iterative algorithms, also known as stochastic approximation (SA) algorithms, for solving fixed-point equations $\bar{F}(x)=x$, where the operator $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ can only be accessed through a noisy oracle. We first focus on the standard setting in which $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ is contractive with respect to some norm and the noise is i.i.d., and explain how generalized Moreau envelopes serve as universal Lyapunov functions, regardless of the underlying norm. We then show how this framework yields mean-square convergence guarantees and applies to stochastic gradient descent, linear SA, and value-based reinforcement learning algorithms such as Q-learning and temporal-difference learning. Finally, we discuss extensions to Markovian noise, seminorm-contractive operators, dissipative operators, and high-probability bounds, and conclude with open problems. The goal is to present a unified and self-contained roadmap for the finite-time analysis of SA and its applications, especially in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [108] [Generalized Intention Modeling in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31318)
*Mateusz Odrowaz-Sypniewski,Jasmine Bayrooti,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种任务自适应的对手建模框架，通过学习多个意图表示的性能驱动混合，并引入一种最大化与自身未来收益互信息的新意图表示，在多种任务中匹配或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手建模方法预先选定某种信息（如对手下一动作或未来环境状态）来编码意图，但经验表明这些信息并非普遍代表意图，因为意图往往依赖于具体任务和环境。

Method: 提出任务自适应对手建模框架，学习性能驱动的多种意图表示的混合；引入新意图表示，最大化与自身未来收益的互信息，从而捕捉与性能最直接相关的对手信息。

Result: 在多种任务中一致匹配或超越当前最先进的基线方法，并揭示了不同对手建模策略何时以及为何成功。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的多意图混合能有效解决对手建模中意图表示选择的问题，互信息最大化的表示能更直接地捕捉与性能相关的对手信息。

Abstract: Modeling an opponent's intent is critical for effective decision-making in non-cooperative, competitive, and general-sum multi-agent reinforcement learning. Existing opponent modeling methods encode intent using an embedding derived from episode information chosen a priori, such as the opponent's next action or a future environment state, and use this to guide the ego-agent's behavior. These approaches assume that the chosen information is universally representative of intent; however, we show empirically that this is not the case as intentions are often task- and environment-dependent. To address this, we introduce a task-adaptive opponent modeling framework that learns a performance-driven mixture of multiple intent representations. We further introduce a new intention representation that maximizes mutual information with the ego-agent's future returns, thereby capturing opponent information that is most directly relevant to performance. Our approach consistently matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art baselines across diverse tasks and yields insights into when and why different opponent modeling strategies succeed.

</details>


### [109] [Trading Complexity for Expressivity Through Structured Generalized Linear Token Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31367)
*Erwan Fagnou,Paul Caillon,Blaise Delattre,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将token混合层分解为直接影响和循环传播两个关键特征，通过引入结构化循环模式（允许状态依赖多个历史状态而非仅前一个），在运行时与表达能力之间实现可控权衡，并在合成任务和语言建模上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token混合层（如注意力机制和状态空间模型）在解码速度、内存需求和缓存大小之间存在必要的权衡，缺乏一个统一的视角来理解和设计新的权衡方案。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，分离两个关键特征：(i) 输入对输出的直接影响；(ii) 通过过去输出进行的循环信息传播。该框架不仅涵盖注意力和状态空间模型，还通过允许每个状态依赖多个历史状态而非仅前一个来泛化循环方程，从而设计出具有可控复杂度和表达性的新循环模式。

Result: 在合成任务和语言建模上进行了经验验证，结果表明新设计的循环模式能按需实现复杂度与表达性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的工具包，用于理解和设计跨模型家族的高效且富有表达力的token混合器。

Abstract: Token mixing layers play a key role in how language models can learn and generate long-range dependencies. Their efficiency relies on the necessary trade-off between decoding speed and the memory requirements, along with the cache size. Considering causal generation, this paper explores new trade-offs thanks to a unified framework which separates two crucial features: (i) the direct influence of inputs on outputs in one generation step; (ii) the recurrent propagation of information through past outputs. This framework encompasses major architectures such as attention and state-space models, but also generalizes the recurrence equations by allowing each state to depend on multiple past states rather than only the immediate predecessor. By introducing structure, we design new recurrence patterns that provably achieve the desired complexity, while providing theoretical insights on their expressivity -- trading runtime for expressivity in a principled way. Empirical validation is performed on synthetic tasks, along with language modeling. Together, these results provide a unified toolkit for the understanding and design of efficient and expressive token mixers across model families.

</details>


### [110] [A Unifying View of Variational Generative Wasserstein Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31369)
*Paul Caucheteux,Clément Bonet,Anna Korba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了生成式Wasserstein流（GWF）的统一理论框架，证明现有多种生成模型可被视为f-散度目标下的参数化JKO格式实例，并将框架扩展到IPM和MMD，推导出新算法并阐明与GAN的关联，最后分析参数化Wasserstein流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型虽均可视为最小化概率分布之间的散度，但依赖的算法和几何原理各不相同，缺乏统一的数学框架来理解和关联这些方法。

Method: 基于Wasserstein梯度流的连续时间公式，通过Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto（JKO）格式的隐式离散化进行近似，建立参数化JKO方案的统一框架，并将其从f-散度扩展到积分概率度量（IPM）和平方最大均值差异（MMD）。

Result: 证明了一类广泛的现有方法可被推导为f-散度目标下参数化JKO格式的实例，建立了若干最近提出算法之间的等价性；推导出基于JKO的新生成算法，并阐明了它们与GAN的联系；实验研究了JKO正则化对不同目标的影响。

Conclusion: GWF提供了一个统一的生成模型理论框架，揭示了不同算法之间的深层联系，并为设计新算法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Many modern generative models can be viewed as minimizing divergences between probability distributions, yet they rely on different algorithmic and geometric principles. Wasserstein gradient flows provide a continuous-time formulation for optimizing over distributions, and can be approximated through their implicit discretization via the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for generative modeling based on Wasserstein gradient flows, which we refer to as Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF). We show that a broad class of existing methods can be derived as instances of parametric JKO schemes for $f$-divergence objectives, and we establish equivalences between several recently proposed algorithms. We extend this framework beyond f-divergence to Integral Probability Metrics and squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy, deriving new JKO-based generative algorithms, and clarifying their connections with GANs. We study empirically the impact of the JKO regularization for a wide set of objectives. Finally, we analyze parametric Wasserstein flows, where the dynamics are restricted to distributions induced by parametrized maps.

</details>


### [111] [Softsign: Smooth Sign in Your Optimizer For Better Parameter Heterogeneity Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31371)
*Dmitrii Feoktistov,Timofey Belinsky,Andrey Veprikov,Amir Zainullin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSignum和SoftMuon，通过软符号变换替代硬符号映射，并辅以自适应分位数温度调度，解决了基于符号的优化器在终端收敛时的振荡问题，在多种深度学习任务（包括大语言模型预训练）上优于硬符号优化器和AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于符号和LMO启发的优化器虽然内存占用低且性能强，但其固定幅度更新机制导致终端收敛性能差：更新与梯度幅度解耦，且未考虑参数异质性，容易产生振荡而非收敛。

Method: 提出SoftSignum，用温度控制的软符号变换替代硬符号映射，实现从符号风格更新到幅度敏感的SGD风格更新的参数级平滑过渡；配合自适应分位数温度调度；将相同原理扩展到矩阵值优化器得到SoftMuon；基于强凸正则化器和Fenchel共轭建立通用几何松弛框架，并证明非凸随机环境下的收敛性。

Result: 在多种深度学习任务（包括大语言模型预训练）上，SoftSignum和SoftMuon一致优于对应的硬符号版本以及标准AdamW优化器。

Conclusion: 基于软符号变换的自适应温度调度策略能有效缓解符号优化器的终端收敛问题，所提出的几何松弛框架具有理论保证和实际有效性。

Abstract: Sign-based and LMO-inspired optimizers have recently attracted substantial attention in deep learning due to their strong performance and low memory footprint. However, their fixed-magnitude updates can hurt terminal convergence: they decouple update mechanisms from gradient magnitudes and fail to account for parameter heterogeneity, often leading to oscillation rather than convergence. We propose SoftSignum, a smooth relaxation of sign-based optimization that replaces the hard sign map with a temperature-controlled soft-sign transformation, enabling a parameter-wise transition from sign-like updates to magnitude-sensitive SGD-like steps. We complement it with an adaptive quantile-based temperature schedule and extend the same principle to matrix-valued optimizers, obtaining SoftMuon. We also develop a generalized geometry-relaxation framework based on strongly convex regularizers and Fenchel conjugates, proving convergence in stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on diverse deep learning tasks, including LLM pretraining, show that SoftSignum and SoftMuon consistently improve over their hard sign-based counterparts and standard AdamW.

</details>


### [112] [Constrained Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Max-Min Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31388)
*Giseung Park,Hyunyoung Nam,Woohyeon Byeon,Amir Leshem,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合最大最小准则与显式约束满足的多目标强化学习框架，通过理论分析、收敛性证明以及在表格环境、建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理中的实验验证，展示了该方法在兼顾公平性与约束满足方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最大最小多目标强化学习在促进公平性方面有效，但在需要满足约束的场景中适用性有限，因此亟需一种能同时处理多目标公平优化与约束满足的框架。

Method: 提出一个将最大最小准则与显式约束整合的MORL框架，从理论上建立该框架的基础，并在表格设置中通过收敛性分析验证算法，同时在建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和交通管理三个实际领域进行实验。

Result: 在表格环境中的收敛性分析验证了算法的理论性质；在建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理三个实际领域，该方法有效平衡了公平性与约束满足。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功将最大最小公平性准则与约束满足相结合，扩展了MORL在需要同时考虑公平性和约束的现实场景中的应用能力。

Abstract: Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) extends standard RL by optimizing policies with respect to multiple, often conflicting, objectives. While max-min MORL has emerged as an effective approach for promoting fairness, its applicability remains limited, particularly when constraints must be incorporated. In this paper, we propose a MORL framework that integrates the max-min criterion with explicit constraint satisfaction. We establish a theoretical foundation for the proposed framework and validate the resulting algorithm through convergence analysis and experiments in tabular settings. We further demonstrate the practical relevance of our approach in simulated building thermal control, multi-objective locomotion control, and greenhouse-gas-emission-aware traffic management. Across these domains, our method effectively balances fairness and constraint satisfaction in multi-objective decision-making.

</details>


### [113] [Fixed Universal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31423)
*Jingwen Liu,Alexandr Andoni,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通用Transformer（Universal Transformers），即通过合适的输入嵌入，固定参数的Transformer可以模拟给定类别中的任何Transformer。类似于通用图灵机，输入嵌入编码了目标模型的描述，而所有内部参数保持固定。作者提供了显式的稀疏构造（在嵌入维度足够大时实现通用性），并进一步证明了通用性是普遍的：随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然具有通用性，这与Zhong和Andreas（2024）的实证结果一致。研究在括号平衡和多跳推理等算法任务上进行了实验验证。结果表明，Transformer的很大一部分表达能力可能存在于输入表示中，而非学习到的权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索固定参数的Transformer是否能通过输入嵌入来模拟任意其他Transformer，从而理解Transformer表达能力的本质来源，特别是区分表达能力来源于模型权重还是输入表示。

Method: 提出通用Transformer的概念，通过构造显式的稀疏嵌入方案实现通用性，并利用随机初始化证明通用性的普遍性（几乎必然成立），最后在括号平衡和多跳推理任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 在嵌入维度足够大时，稀疏构造可以实现通用性；随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然具有通用性；实验验证了理论的有效性。

Conclusion: Transformer的大部分表达能力可能存在于输入表示中，而非学习到的权重，这改变了对模型表达能力来源的传统理解。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{universal transformers}: fixed transformers that can simulate any transformer in a given class via a suitable input embedding. Analogous to a universal Turing machine, the input embedding encodes a description of the target model while all internal parameters remain fixed. We provide explicit sparse constructions achieving universality when the embedding dimension is sufficiently large, and further show that universality is generic: randomly initialized transformers are universal almost surely, which aligns with recent empirical results of Zhong and Andreas (2024). We empirically validate our theory on the algorithmic tasks of parenthesis balancing and multi-hop reasoning. Our results suggest that much of a transformer's expressive power may reside in its input representation rather than its learned weights.

</details>


### [114] [Flow map learning in nonlinear vector autoregressive models: influence of the feature-library structure on the training error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31438)
*Markus Gross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性向量自回归（NVAR/NG-RC）模型在时间序列预测中的可辨识性问题，揭示了训练误差随时间分辨率变化的标度律，并指出即使训练误差很小，若模型类与真实过程不匹配，泛化能力仍然较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测常需要学习非线性和时滞依赖关系，而NVAR/NG-RC作为一类重要模型，其可辨识性（即能否从数据中唯一且正确地识别潜在动力学）尚未被充分理解，特别是训练误差与泛化能力之间的关系不明确。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究NVAR/NG-RC模型训练误差作为时间分辨率函数的（预）渐近标度律，区分特征库能否精确表示流映射（传播子）的早期Lie级数系数两种情况。对多项式向量场驱动的动力系统，使用单项式和傅里叶特征库进行验证，并考察时间分辨率、非线性度、延迟项数的影响。

Result: 训练误差随时间分辨率呈特征标度律；延迟项可降低最优单步训练误差，但仅当特征库提供足够非线性时才改善长期预测；小训练误差与弱泛化可能共存（模型类不匹配时）。多种混沌动力系统的数值实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: NVAR/NG-RC模型的训练误差存在可预测的标度行为，但低训练误差并不保证良好的长期预测能力，模型类与真实动力学的匹配度是关键。这为设计特征库和选择延迟项提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Time series forecasting often requires learning nonlinear and time-delayed dependencies. A paradigmatic class of forecasting models are nonlinear vector autoregressive processes (NVAR), also known as next-generation reservoir computers (NG-RCs). These models approximate the Koopman operator on the space spanned by their explicit feature library. We consider the identifiability problem for learning Markovian nonlinear dynamical systems and show that the training error as a function of time resolution follows characteristic (pre-)asymptotic scaling laws. These laws depend on whether the feature library can represent the early Lie-series coefficients of the flow map (propagator) exactly or merely approximately. For dynamical systems governed by polynomial vector fields, we demonstrate the mechanism for NVAR/NG-RC models with monomial and Fourier feature libraries. We determine the dependence of the training error on the temporal resolution, the involved nonlinear degree, and the number of delay terms. While delay terms reduce the optimal one-step training error, they improve long-horizon forecasts only when the library provides sufficient nonlinearity. Thus, small training error coexists with weak generalization as the model class is mismatched to the true data-generating process. Numerical experiments on various chaotic dynamical systems confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [115] [DRIFT: Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Efficient Multi-Turn Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31455)
*Jian Mu,Tianyi Lin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT框架通过解耦轨迹采样与优化，将KL正则化RL目标转化为重要性加权监督学习，在保持SFT效率的同时匹配甚至超越多轮RL基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互场景中，在线RL因需生成完整校正轨迹而成本高昂，离线SFT虽高效但面临分布偏移和行为崩溃问题，亟需一种既高效又能处理多轮动态的优化方法。

Method: 提出DRIFT框架：从固定参考策略离线采样交互轨迹，基于回报计算重要性权重，通过加权监督微调优化策略，实现解耦的轨迹生成与策略优化。

Result: 实验表明DRIFT在多轮强化学习基线中达到匹配或超越的性能，同时保持标准SFT的训练效率和简洁性。

Conclusion: DRIFT有效弥合了在线RL与离线SFT之间的差距，为多轮交互场景提供了一种实用且高效的优化范式。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multi-turn interactive settings where users or environments can iteratively provide lightweight feedback. Unfortunately, optimizing such behavior presents a sharp dilemma in practice: online reinforcement learning is able to effectively address multi-turn dynamics but is prohibitively expensive due to the cost of generating full correction trajectories at every update, whereas offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is efficient but suffers from distribution shift and behavioral collapse. To this end, we novelly propose DRIFT (Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning), a framework that operationalizes the theoretical insight that the KL-regularized RL objective is equivalent to importance-weighted supervised learning. DRIFT decouples rollout from optimization by sampling offline interaction trajectories from a fixed reference policy, deriving return-based importance weights, and optimizing the policy via weighted SFT on the resulting dataset. Empirically, we demonstrate that DRIFT matches or exceeds the performance of multi-turn reinforcement learning baselines while maintaining the training efficiency and simplicity of standard supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/DRIFT.

</details>


### [116] [Balanced LoRA: Removing Parameter Invariance to Accelerate Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31484)
*Valérie Castin,Kimia Nadjahi,Pierre Ablin,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA存在过参数化问题，不同低秩因子对的条件数差异大，影响收敛速度。BaLoRA通过投影到平衡流形改善条件数，实现更快收敛和更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽然被广泛使用，但其过参数化特性导致不同低秩因子对的收敛速度差异显著，影响微调效率。

Method: 提出BaLoRA（Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation），通过将迭代投影到平衡流形上，在保持适应矩阵不变的前提下改善损失曲面的条件数。该投影计算轻量，易于集成到现有微调流程中。

Result: BaLoRA比标准LoRA收敛更快，在多个微调任务上取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 平衡低秩因子对的投影操作能有效改善损失曲面的条件数，从而加速收敛并提升微调效果。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is the most widely adopted method for fine-tuning large language models. Notably, LoRA is inherently overparameterized: multiple pairs of low-rank factors can yield the same adapted weight matrix. We show--both theoretically and empirically--that these pairs exhibit significantly different condition numbers. As a result, converging to different loss minimizers directly impacts the convergence rate of LoRA. Building on this observation, we introduce Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation (BaLoRA), a variant of LoRA that projects iterates onto a balanced manifold. This manifold improves the conditioning of the loss landscape while preserving the adapted matrix. The projection step is computationally lightweight and integrates seamlessly into existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirically, BaLoRA converges faster than standard LoRA and achieves superior performance across a range of fine-tuning tasks.

</details>


### [117] [Assign and Add: A Mechanistic Study of Compositional Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31497)
*Brady Exoo,Alberto Bietti,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在变量赋值和模加法的受控实验中，揭示了小型Transformer实现组合泛化的机制：同一模加法MLP模块可被直接或间接输入共用，训练动力学分为三个阶段（先学模加法、再学变量赋值结构、最后精炼泛化），并从理论上解释了组合性如何从训练动态中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer中组合泛化（compositional generalization）的具体机制，理解模型如何将已学技能组合以处理训练中未见过的新组合任务。

Method: 构建变量赋值与模加法的受控实验，将训练数据划分为不相交集合；通过机制分析（mechanistic analysis）定位模型内部模块的复用情况；从经验角度分析训练动力学；提供理论框架解释组合性的涌现。

Result: 小型Transformer能泛化到未见过的变量与数字组合；同一模加法MLP模块在处理直接输入和间接变量赋值输入时被复用；训练呈现三阶段：模加法学习→变量赋值结构学习→精炼泛化阶段。

Conclusion: 组合泛化可以是Transformer内部机制组合性的自然结果，其涌现可通过训练动力学得到理论解释。

Abstract: Large language models are able to compose skills in order to perform complex tasks, many of which might not have been seen during training. The details of how exactly this composition occurs remain elusive. In this paper, we study a mechanism for compositional generalization in transformers by considering a simple controlled setting involving variable assignment and modular addition. By partitioning our training data into disjoint sets, we observe that small transformers are able to generalize to previously unseen combinations of variables and numbers. Our mechanistic analysis shows that the same ``modular addition'' MLP module is used whether the inputs are given directly or indirectly through a separate variable assignment mechanism. We also analyze the training dynamics from an empirical lens, which reveals three phases of learning: first, modular addition is learned, then the structure required for variable assignment, and finally a refinement phase where the model generalizes to some hard sequences not seen in training. Finally, we provide a theoretical framework to explain how compositionality emerges from training dynamics. These results suggest that compositional generalization can be a natural consequence of the compositionality of internal mechanisms in~transformers.

</details>


### [118] [When Are Multimodal Predictions Biologically Supported? A Diagnostic Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31504)
*Dylan Steiner,Gustavo Arango-Argoty,Gerald Sun,Etai Jacob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DECAT框架，用于多模态肿瘤学模型中区分共享生物学、单模态生物学和虚假相关性的后验评估方法，在合成数据和真实TCGA数据上验证，发现CLIP等纠缠模型存在高假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态肿瘤学模型虽然预测准确，但无法判断模型学到的是跨模态共享的生物学知识、单一模态特有的生物学知识，还是反映混杂因素的虚假相关性。

Method: 提出DECAT框架，包含五个零基准指标和基于规则的决策程序，对多模态表示进行分类诊断，无需预先知道混杂因素。

Result: 在合成数据（超2500个训练表示）和真实TCGA数据（8979名患者）上验证；发现CLIP等纠缠模型共享生物学检测近乎完美，但在多数实际基础模型嵌入中误报共享生物学，且误报率随混杂强度增加。

Conclusion: DECAT能检测AUROC无法识别的混杂因素，且无需混杂标签，后验分层分析证实其有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal models in oncology can produce accurate predictions, but accurate prediction does not reveal whether the model has learned biology that is shared across modalities, biology confined to one modality, or spurious correlations that reflect confounders rather than genuine biology. We introduce DECAT, a model-agnostic post-hoc evaluation framework that classifies multimodal representations into four diagnostic scenarios for a given task and modality, using five null-referenced metrics and a rule-based decision procedure. The framework operates on learned representations, requires no knowledge of which specific confounder is present, and returns indeterminate when the evidence is insufficient. We validate DECAT on synthetic data across four multimodal model classes (over 2,500 trained representations) and on real data from 8,979 TCGA patients, evaluating both multimodal embeddings and five pretrained pathology foundation models. Entangled models (e.g., CLIP) achieve near-perfect shared biology detection but falsely claim shared biology in the majority of cases where it is absent on real foundation model embeddings. This false claim rate increases with confound strength so that larger cohorts and stronger representations produce more confident but still incorrect diagnoses. Applied to both multimodal TCGA embeddings and five pathology foundation models without paired RNA, DECAT detects confounding invisible to AUROC without requiring the confounder labels, as confirmed by post-hoc stratification.

</details>


### [119] [On the Relationship Between Activation Outliers and Feature Death in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31518)
*Elana Simon,Etowah Adams,James Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏自编码器(SAE)中特征死亡问题源于激活层中的维度级异常值，提出用γ=‖μ‖/‖σ‖量化异常严重程度，并证明均值中心化预处理可消除异常值导致的特征死亡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE训练中许多学习到的特征永远不会激活（特征死亡），浪费字典容量并可能重新引入叠加现象。不同模型间死亡率差异巨大（GPT-2接近0%，AlphaFold3超过70%），需要理解其根本机制。

Method: 提出维度级激活异常值的概念：维度均值相对于每个token的变异较大时产生异常值。形式化异常严重程度为γ=‖μ‖/‖σ‖。通过实验验证γ与初始死亡率的相关性（Spearman ρ=0.89 for TopK, 0.82 for ReLU），并测试均值中心化预处理的效果。

Result: γ可预测454个模型-层组合中的初始死亡率。死特征在训练中可以复活，但需要SAE偏置学习激活均值，在高γ下过程极慢。均值中心化消除了所有测试模型中的异常值诱导死亡。

Conclusion: 确认了特征死亡的机制源于激活异常值与特征对齐方向的相互作用，为均值中心化预处理提供了理论依据和必要性条件。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose neural network activations into interpretable features, but many learned features never activate, a problem called feature death that wastes dictionary capacity and can reintroduce superposition. Death rates vary dramatically between models: near-zero on GPT-2, over 70% on AlphaFold3 with identical configurations. We find that dimension-level activation outliers (dimensions whose mean magnitude is large relative to per-token variation) cause this by shifting pre-activations at initialization based on each feature's alignment with the activation mean. Features anti-aligned with the mean receive permanently negative pre-activations and never fire. We formalize outlier severity as $γ= \|μ\|/\|σ\|$; it predicts initial death rates (Spearman $ρ= 0.89$ for dead-by-TopK, $0.82$ for dead-by-ReLU) across 454 model-layer combinations spanning language, vision, protein, and genomic models. Dead features can revive during training, but recovery requires the SAE bias to learn the activation mean, a process that is prohibitively slow at high $γ$. Mean-centering (subtracting the activation mean) sidesteps this and eliminates outlier-induced death across all tested models, confirming the mechanism and providing a principled basis for when and why this preprocessing step is necessary.

</details>


### [120] [Chem-PerturBridge: a harmonized compendium of small molecule perturbation transcriptomic effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31522)
*Artur Szałata,Olga Novitskaia,Maiia Shulman,Matthew Mella,Altynbek Zhubanchaliyev,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chem-PerturBridge是一个整合了37k+化合物、136种细胞背景、125万转录组样本的跨数据集资源，支持小分子建模的预训练和跨数据集评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于小分子建模的转录组资源在技术平台、元数据规范、对照、剂量和预处理流程上分散割裂，缺乏统一、大规模、多样化的训练资源。

Method: 整合多个数据集，统一标识符、元数据，并计算考虑重复的condition-level效应量；在此基础上评估跨数据集的匹配条件一致性和模型预训练效果。

Result: 匹配条件的logFC排序和幅度跨数据集一致性弱（常低于同背景不同化合物的基线），但方向一致性更稳定且超过基线；Chem-PerturBridge预训练的嵌入在化合物留出评估中优于L1000-only、Morgan指纹和OP3基线。

Conclusion: Chem-PerturBridge同时支持跨数据集签名一致性的诊断评估和异构扰动转录组数据的模型复用预训练。

Abstract: Large perturbation models require training data encompassing chemical, cellular, and assay diversity. Current transcriptomic resources for small-molecule modeling, however, are fragmented across technologies, metadata conventions, controls, doses, and preprocessing pipelines. We introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized multi-dataset resource comprising over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types, with standardized identifiers, metadata, and replicate-aware condition-level effects. We use the resource to evaluate matched-condition agreement across datasets and replicate agreement within datasets. Matched same-compound conditions generally show weak agreement in fine-grained logFC rankings and magnitudes across most dataset pairs, often falling below same-context different-compound baselines. In contrast, logFC direction agreement is substantially more stable and usually exceeds these baselines. We further evaluate Chem-PerturBridge as a pretraining resource for compound representation learning. Under a compound-held-out OP3 evaluation split, embeddings pretrained on Chem-PerturBridge improve over L1000-only embeddings, Morgan fingerprints, and the descriptor-free OP3 baseline across metrics. An extensive molecule-holdout evaluation across 11 datasets further shows that models trained on Chem-PerturBridge outperform or match those that are not. Chem-PerturBridge therefore supports both diagnostic evaluation of cross-dataset signature agreement and model-oriented reuse of heterogeneous perturbation transcriptomic data.

</details>


### [121] [Value Functions as Supermartingale Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31524)
*Alessandro Abate,Daniel Contro,Mirco Giacobbe,Agustín Martínez-Suñé,Diptarko Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了强化学习与随机系统形式化验证之间的理论联系：在适当奖励函数下，几乎必然满足ω-正则规范策略的价值函数编码了Streett超鞅证书，为通过RL进行证书合成提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合两条研究路线之间的鸿沟：一是基于超鞅证书的随机系统形式化验证方法（可处理一般状态空间），二是ω-正则任务的强化学习方法（通常缺乏形式化保证）。

Method: 建立了理论连接：证明在适当奖励设计下，几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的策略所对应的价值函数，编码了一个Streett超鞅证书，从而将RL与形式化验证统一起来。

Result: 该理论结果对有穷、可数无穷和连续状态空间均成立，并在有穷马尔可夫决策过程上得到了实验验证。

Conclusion: 提出了一条通过强化学习实现证书合成的原则性路径，使RL方法能够为ω-正则规范提供形式化保证。

Abstract: Certification methods for stochastic systems provide sufficient proof rules, based on real-valued supermartingale certificates, to determine the almost-sure satisfaction of $ω$-regular properties (and therefore of linear temporal logic) over general state spaces, encompassing both countably infinite and continuous state spaces. Conversely, reinforcement learning (RL) methods for $ω$-regular tasks have received considerable attention, but they typically lack formal guarantees that the learned policy satisfies the specification, except possibly for finite state and action spaces. We bridge these two lines of research by establishing a novel theoretical connection: under an appropriate reward, the value function associated to a policy that almost surely satisfies an $ω$-regular property encodes a Streett supermartingale certificate for that specification. Our results, validated experimentally on finite Markov decision processes, hold for finite, countably infinite, and continuous state spaces, suggesting a principled route to certificate synthesis via RL.

</details>


### [122] [The Dynamic-Probabilistic Consistency Gap in Chaotic Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31547)
*Andre Herz,Matthijs Pals,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了动态系统重构中动态-概率一致性差距（DPC gap），即有限时域概率目标会损害动力学特征或使预测不确定性与局部切线动力学解耦，并提出基于可微扩展卡尔曼滤波的KAFFEE框架来弥合这一差距，在随机超混沌Lorenz-96和13个混沌系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态系统重构（DSR）旨在从时间序列数据中学习替代模型以捕捉底层动力学，但现有方法在部署时需要与所学动力学一致的不确定性估计。作者发现追求有限时域概率目标会损害动力学特征或使预测不确定性与局部切线动力学解耦（称为DPC gap），需要解决这一问题。

Method: 提出KAFFEE（Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation）框架，采用可微扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）训练方法，在局部预测残差（新息）上评估似然，同时通过学习的局部雅可比矩阵传输协方差，从而在训练中保持动态-概率一致性。

Result: 在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统上，KAFFEE减少了核心坍缩、噪声掩蔽和盲不确定性三种失效模式，改善了动力学不变量的重构，并保持了有竞争力的预测得分。在13个混沌系统上，KAFFEE实现了上下文贝叶斯滤波，同时大致保持了零样本动力学特性。

Conclusion: 论文识别并解决了DSR中的DPC gap问题，证明开环高斯 rollout 目标会惩罚混沌系统中的雅可比生成协方差增长，促使优化捷径削弱物理扩展或使其与不确定性解耦。KAFFEE框架通过EKF-based训练有效弥合了这一差距，实现了更可靠的动态系统重构。

Abstract: Dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) aims to learn surrogate models that capture the dynamics underlying time-series data. Reliably deploying these surrogates requires uncertainty estimates consistent with the learned dynamics. We expose a dynamic-probabilistic consistency (DPC) gap: the pursuit of finite-horizon probabilistic objectives can degrade dynamics or decouple predictive uncertainty from the local tangent dynamics it ought to reflect. We isolate three mechanisms behind this gap: core collapse, noise masking, and blind uncertainty. Specifically, we show that open-loop Gaussian rollout objectives can penalize Jacobian-generated covariance growth in chaotic systems, encouraging optimization shortcuts that weaken physical expansion or decouple uncertainty from it. To mitigate this gap, we propose KAFFEE (Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation), a differentiable extended Kalman filter-based training framework that evaluates likelihood on local predictive residuals (innovations) while transporting covariance through learned local Jacobians. On stochastic hyperchaotic Lorenz-96, KAFFEE reduces the identified failure modes, improves reconstruction of dynamical invariants relative to open-loop objectives, and maintains competitive predictive scores. We further show that the DPC gap appears when probabilistically adapting a DSR foundation model across 13 chaotic systems, where KAFFEE enables in-context Bayesian filtering while largely preserving zero-shot dynamics.

</details>


### [123] [Functional Attention: From Pairwise Affinities to Functional Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31559)
*Jiefang Xiao,Maolin Gao,Simon Weber,Guandao Yang,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Functional Attention，一种将注意力机制重新解释为自适应基之间函数对应关系的方法，受几何函数映射启发，用结构化线性算子替代softmax亲和度，实现紧凑、可泛化、分辨率不变的算子学习，在PDE求解、3D分割和回归等任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的算子学习方法将连续场视为离散token，通常忽略全局函数结构，缺乏对无限维函数空间之间映射的有效建模能力。

Method: 提出Functional Attention，将注意力机制重新解释为自适应基之间的函数对应关系，受几何函数映射启发，用结构化线性算子替代softmax亲和度，从而获得紧凑、可泛化、分辨率不变的表示，显式捕获全局依赖关系。

Result: 在多个算子学习任务（包括求解PDE、3D分割和回归）中达到与最先进方法相当的性能，同时对不同离散化方式保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Functional Attention通过将注意力机制从离散token匹配提升为连续函数空间之间的映射，为算子学习提供了一种更结构化和可泛化的范式，在保持分辨率不变性的同时实现了SOTA性能。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional function spaces, or operator learning, is essential for many machine learning applications. Although transformer-based operators are popular, they often rely on token-wise attention. These methods treat continuous fields as discrete tokens and usually ignore the global functional structure. We introduce \emph{Functional Attention}, which reinterprets attention as a functional correspondence between adaptive bases. Inspired by geometric functional maps, our method replaces softmax affinities with structured linear operators. This yields a compact, generalizable, resolution-invariant representation that explicitly captures global dependencies. Experiments demonstrate that \emph{Functional Attention} can match state-of-the-art performance in many operator learning tasks, including solving PDEs, 3D segmentation, and regression, while remaining robust to varying discretizations. Project page is available at https://github.com/xjffff/FUNCATTN.

</details>


### [124] [Effective Biological Representation Learning by Masking Gene Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31562)
*Kian Kenyon-Dean,Alina Selega,Ihab Bendidi,Jordan M. Sorokin,Luca Bertinetto,David Errington,Hayley Donnella,Oren Kraus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TxFM模型，通过掩码自编码方法处理RNA-seq数据，在精心整理的DiverseRNA-1.4M数据集上训练，证明了归纳式自监督学习在转录组学表征中的有效性，其性能优于在超大语料库上训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转录组基础模型在深度表征学习方面表现不佳，甚至不如线性基线，这引发了对深度学习在转录组数据中是否具有优势的质疑。本文旨在探索归纳式自监督学习能否为转录组学表示提供切实优势。

Method: 提出TxFM模型，采用适用于多样化RNA-seq计数数据的掩码自编码方法；通过消融研究确定关键架构配置；并构建了公开训练语料DiverseRNA-1.4M。

Result: TxFM在精心整理的数据集上训练，产生了高保真基因表征，其性能优于在超过其100倍大小的图谱级语料库上训练的基础模型。

Conclusion: 归纳式自监督学习是转录组学表示的一种可行建模方法，但需要精心设计模型架构和训练数据 curation。

Abstract: RNA sequencing produces rich and diverse datasets of gene expression, offering compelling insights into cellular state and function that have many applications in drug discovery. Modeling such data is challenging due to inherent technical noise and experimental batch effects, as evidenced by many existing transcriptomic foundation models (FMs) underperforming relative to linear baselines. Such results raise the question of whether deep representation learning provides a distinct advantage over the direct use of raw transcript counts. Our work explores this by developing a new self-supervised model, TxFM, with a focus on inductive representation learning evaluations. TxFM employs a masked autoencoding approach tailored to diverse RNA-seq count data, and our ablation study empirically identifies crucial architecture configurations required for strong transfer performance. Additionally, we curate a public training corpus, DiverseRNA-1.4M, and find that TxFM trained on this curated dataset yields high-fidelity gene representations that outperform FMs trained on atlas-scale corpora over 100x larger. Overall, our results indicate that inductive self-supervised learning is a viable modeling approach for transcriptomics representation, provided a careful synthesis of model architecture and training data curation.

</details>


### [125] [Giving Sensors a Voice: Multimodal JEPA for Semantic Time-Series Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31580)
*Utsav Dutta,Gerardo Pastrana,Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad,Henrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CHARM模型，利用通道级文本描述和JEPA架构，在异构多元时间序列上学习可泛化的通道感知表示，仅用线性探针即可在多种任务上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用异构多元时间序列的表示学习尚未被充分探索，现有Transformer方法多针对语言/视觉，缺乏对多变量时间序列中通道间关系及跨数据集泛化的有效建模。

Method: 将通道级文本描述与Transformer编码器结合，保持对通道顺序的等变性；采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）进行训练，提出新的损失函数以促进信息丰富且时间稳定的嵌入；利用描述感知门控机制学习通道间关系，增强可解释性。

Result: 在异常检测、分类、短期与长期预测任务上，仅使用线性探针（linear probe）即可取得强性能。性能提升主要来自JEPA目标和条件架构，文本描述作为通道标识符促进跨数据集泛化。

Conclusion: CHARM证明将通道文本描述引入Transformer结合JEPA预训练，是学习通用多元时间序列表示的有效范式，兼顾性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have advanced sequence modeling in language and vision, yet general-purpose representation learning for heterogeneous multivariate time series remains underexplored. We introduce CHARM (Channel-Aware Representation Model), which incorporates channel-level textual descriptions into a Transformer encoder equivariant to channel order. CHARM is trained with a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and a novel loss promoting informative, temporally stable embeddings; latent-space prediction encourages robustness to sensor noise while description-aware gating provides interpretability through learned inter-channel relationships. Across anomaly detection, classification, and short- and long-term forecasting, the learned embeddings achieve strong performance using only a linear probe. Performance is driven primarily by the JEPA objective and conditioning architecture, with text descriptions serving as channel identifiers for cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [126] [A Tight Theory of Error Feedback Algorithms in Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31594)
*Daniel Berg Thomsen,Adrien Taylor,Aymeric Dieuleveut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式学习中的梯度压缩误差反馈机制，对EF和EF21两种主流算法进行了紧致收敛性分析，通过确定最优步长选择和构造最优Lyapunov函数，得到了与智能体数量无关且恢复单智能体最优保证的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中通信成本是主要瓶颈，梯度压缩会损害收敛保证，误差反馈机制虽可缓解但多种变体的相对性能尚不明确。

Method: 对EF和EF21两种误差反馈算法进行紧致收敛性分析，通过识别最优步长选择和构造针对每种方法定制的最优Lyapunov函数。

Result: 获得了与智能体数量无关的收敛保证，并在单智能体场景下恢复了已知的最优保证。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析明确了两种主流误差反馈算法的最优参数选择和收敛特性，为分布式优化中梯度压缩的误差反馈机制提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Communication costs are a major bottleneck in distributed learning and first-order optimization. A common approach to alleviate this issue is to compress the gradient information exchanged between agents. However, such compression typically degrades the convergence guarantees of gradient-based methods. Error feedback mechanisms provide a simple and computationally cheap remedy for this issue, but numerous variants have been proposed, and their relative performance remains poorly understood. This paper provides tight convergence analyses for two of the main error-feedback algorithms from the literature, the classic Error Feedback method (EF) and Error Feedback 21 (EF21), by identifying optimal step-size choices and constructing optimal Lyapunov functions tailored to each method. The results hold independently of the number of agents and recover the known best guarantees possible in the single-agent regime.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Polynomial Histograms for Memory-Efficient Representation of Long-tailed System Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30360)
*Murray Stokely,Tim Hesterberg,Arif Merchant,Nate Coehlo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分箱数据的信息损失度量方法，通过多项式直方图（每个箱标注矩信息）与传统直方图在相同存储成本下进行对比，并在大规模生产系统的文件系统指标中验证了该方法在更低成本下提供更多信息的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统需要在多台计算机上跟踪多种性能指标（如延迟分布），这些经验分布需要以最小代价收集、高效跨维度聚合，并以紧凑形式存储以支持下游数据分析。现有方法在信息损失与存储成本之间难以取得最优平衡。

Method: 定义了一种针对分箱数据的信息损失度量指标；提出多项式直方图，为每个箱标注底层分布的矩信息；在相同存储成本下，将多项式直方图与传统直方图（使用额外箱而非箱内标注）进行对比；在大规模生产系统的文件系统指标上应用并分析性能。

Result: 通过理论分析和实际应用，表征了在何种条件下多项式直方图能以更低成本提供更多信息，相比传统直方图更具优势。

Conclusion: 多项式直方图在存储成本受限的场景下，通过箱内矩标注而非增加箱数，能够更高效地保留分布信息，适用于分布式系统性能指标的收集与存储。

Abstract: Distributed systems must frequently keep track of many different types of performance metrics across many different computers. For example, the latency distribution of certain operations may be computed for a large combination of computers, users, and operations. These empirical distributions need to be collected at minimal expense on the individual software components, efficiently aggregated across multiple dimensions, and stored in a compact representation for a variety of downstream data analysis applications.
  We describe an information loss metric for binned data that allows us to optimize cost of information loss from different histogram representations. We explore the use of polynomial histograms where each bin of a histogram is annotated with moments of the underlying distribution in that bin. These polynomial histograms are compared to traditional histograms using the same storage cost for additional bins instead of annotations in each bin. We describe an application of these techniques for file system metrics for a large production system, and analytically characterize when polynomial histograms offer more information at lower cost.

</details>


### [128] [A Datalog Framework for Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31569)
*Elena Yanakieva,Annette Bieniusa,Stefania Dumbrava*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Datalog的声明式框架，用于规约和推理CRDT（无冲突复制数据类型）及其应用的语义，并通过属性基测试验证实现正确性，在协同图数据编辑案例中展示了其可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式应用越来越多地支持本地优先的协同编辑，允许多用户无需全局协调即可并发更新。这种协同需要精心设计以捕获并发交互的预期语义，但现有方法缺乏系统化、可组合的语义分析与验证手段。

Method: 引入一个基于Datalog的声明式框架，将CRDT语义建模为可执行的逻辑程序（在操作上下文上），使并发显式化且可组合，从而支持自动化分析。利用属性基测试（property-based testing）比较不同实现的正确性。

Result: 通过一个协同图数据编辑案例研究，评估了该方法的正确性验证效果和随操作数量与副本数量增长的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性地使用Datalog作为基础来原型设计和分析复杂CRDT及其组合的工作，证明了该框架在语义规约、自动化验证和可扩展性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Distributed applications increasingly support local-first collaboration over shared data, allowing multiple users to perform updates concurrently without global coordination. Such collaboration requires careful design to capture the intended semantics of the concurrent interactions.
  We introduce a declarative framework for specifying and reasoning about the semantics of conflict-free replicated data types (CRDTs) and CRDT-based applications in Datalog. The framework models CRDT semantics as executable logic programs over operation contexts, making concurrency explicit and compositional, and thus amenable to automated analysis. As one application, we use property-based testing to compare implementations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically use Datalog as a foundation for prototyping and analyzing complex CRDTs and their compositions.
  We evaluate our methodology using a collaborative graph data editing case study and report experimentation results assessing correctness validation and scalability with an increasing number of operations and replicas.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [PhyDrawGen: Physically Grounded Diagram Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30512)
*Nafiul Haque,Syed Nazmus Sakib,Shifat E Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen 是一个神经符号管道，通过解耦语义理解与物理约束满足，从文本生成物理准确的 diagrams，在涵盖力学、光学和电磁学的 1449 个问题基准上显著优于 GPT-5-image 等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽然在视觉上合理，但系统性地产生幻觉（如错误力向量）、忽略守恒定律和违反几何约束，无法保证物理准确性。

Method: 三阶段神经符号管道：(1) 大语言模型提取类型化场景图；(2) 确定性求解器将场景图转换为平面直线图 (PSLG)，编码力平衡、光路和场拓扑为精确几何基元；(3) 微调 Qwen-VL 模型执行视觉化的 propose-verify 循环，迭代修正约束违反。

Result: 在 1449 个问题（力学、光学、电磁学）基准上，PhyDrawGen 显著优于 GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 Gemini 3 Pro，在罕见物体问题上也表现出稳健的物理准确性。

Conclusion: 将符号推理与神经视觉模型结合的神经符号方法能有效保证物理原理图的精确性，为科学可视化中的可信生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>


### [130] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe,Stepan Tretiakov,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Su Ann Low,Xingjian Li,Hassan Iqbal,Neel P. Bhatt,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有具身AI的观测预测型世界模型会产生物理上不可行的预测，并提出应构建“物理可行”的世界模型——即识别足够回答干预查询的最简物理抽象，通过模块化组件（环境表征、潜状态估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询响应）和自主编排器动态组合，确保模型在干预查询下的结构正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测预测型世界模型尽管能生成视觉上合理的未来预测，但在物理上常常是错误的，导致推荐不可行动作、错误预测交互结果或认证不安全行为。原因是物理系统间存在“观测等价但动力学不同”的结构性问题，单纯拟合观测数据无法捕捉因果干预下的真实行为。

Method: 提出了一种模块化的物理可行世界模型设计原则：模型应包含环境表征、潜状态与参数估计、动作规范、干预动力学和查询级响应五个模块；由自主编排器根据具体查询识别所需的最简物理抽象，动态组合学习的、解析的或混合的转换模型；当封闭形式物理不可用时，可采用解析、仿真、学习或混合的转换模型，但必须保留决定干预结果的结构。

Result: 通过控制变量基准实验（固定视觉场景、变化潜物理参数）暴露了现有模型的失败案例，并展示了所提方法在现有系统无法正确回答的查询上取得了正确结果。

Conclusion: 正确的世界模型抽象不是最详细的模型，而是能保留查询相关区分的最简模型。该分解使模型可解释、组件可验证、输出可审计，为规划、控制和验证提供了动态组装物理可行模型的设计原则。

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>


### [131] [Transforming and Encoding FTS for SAT Solving: What Helps, What Hurts (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30563)
*João Filipe,Álvaro Torralba,Gregor Behnke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将因子化任务（factored tasks）编码为SAT问题，提出了多种将因子化转移关系转化为命题逻辑的编码策略，并分析了在不同层次利用并行性的方法以及常见任务变换对基于SAT的规划器性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化任务是一种比STRIPS或SAS+更紧凑的规划表示形式，但现有规划方法仅局限于启发式搜索。为了拓展其求解途径，本文探索将因子化任务编码为SAT问题，以利用SAT求解器的强大能力。

Method: 提出了多种将因子化转移关系翻译为命题逻辑的编码策略；分析了不同层次并行性的利用方式；研究了常见任务变换对SAT规划器性能的影响。

Result: 给出了多种SAT编码方案，并分析了并行性和任务变换对性能的影响。

Conclusion: 将因子化任务编码为SAT是可行的，不同的编码策略和并行性利用方式对性能有显著影响，任务变换也会影响SAT规划器的表现。

Abstract: Factored tasks are a classical planning representation that extends SAS+ with limited forms of disjunctive preconditions, conditional effects, and angelic nondeterminism. This allows for a more compact representation of tasks than traditional formalisms such as STRIPS or SAS+, and supports a wide range of task transformations. However, existing planning approaches for factored tasks have been limited to heuristic search methods.
  In this work, we investigate how to encode factored tasks in SAT. We propose several ways to encode the tasks, focusing on different strategies for translating the factored transition relation into propositional logic. We also analyze how to exploit parallelism at various levels in this setting and study the impact of common task transformations on the performance of SAT-based planners.

</details>


### [132] [Procedural Generation of First Person Shooter Maps using Map-Elites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30570)
*Simone de Donato,Pier Luca Lanzi,Daniele Loiacono*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究应用MAP-Elites（一种质量多样性算法）为第一人称射击（FPS）游戏设计关卡。提出了两种新地图表征方法（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout），相比传统方法能生成更具多样性和更高质量的FPS地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPS地图设计方法缺乏对地图多样性和质量的系统优化，需要一种能够同时探索多样性和保持高质量的地图生成方法。

Method: 1) 提出两种新颖的FPS地图表征：Point-Line和Spatial-Layout；2) 定义一系列度量指标描述地图的拓扑属性（依赖布局）和涌现属性（通过实际游戏评估）；3) 应用MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB)算法进化FPS地图种群。

Result: 新提出的地图表征（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout）相比传统方法（All-Black和Grid-Graph）能够生成多样性更高且质量更好的FPS地图。

Conclusion: 新的地图表征方法能有效提升MAP-Elites在FPS关卡设计中的表现，产生更优的地图种群。

Abstract: We investigate the application of MAP-Elites (a well-known quality diversity algorithm) to design levels for First-Person Shooter (FPS) games. We consider two well-known map representations (All-Black and Grid-Graph) and introduce two novel representations (Point-Line and Spatial-Layout) that improve the characterization of FPS maps. We define a series of metrics to describe maps' topological properties (which solely depend on maps' layout), and emergent properties (which must be evaluated through actual gameplay). We perform an in-depth analysis to identify the most suitable features to guide MAP-Elites illumination process. We apply MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB) to evolve populations of FPS maps. Our results show that the new representations can generate maps with higher diversity and quality than the representations previously used for evolving FPS maps.

</details>


### [133] [Harness Updating Is Not Harness Benefit: Disentangling Evolution Capabilities in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30621)
*Minhua Lin,Juncheng Wu,Zijun Wang,Zhan Shi,Yisi Sang,Bing He,Zewen Liu,Tianxin Wei,Zongyu Wu,Zhiwei Zhang,Dakuo Wang,Xiang Zhang,Benoit Dumoulin,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou,Suhang Wang,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体在可编辑外部框架（提示、技能、记忆、工具）下的自我演化能力，发现：（1）框架更新能力与模型基础能力呈扁平关系——不同能力层级的模型产生的框架更新带来的性能提升惊人相似；（2）框架受益能力与基础能力呈非单调关系——弱模型受益极少，中等模型受益最大，强模型受益反而低于中等模型。弱模型低收益归因于无法激活相关框架组件或无法忠实遵循框架指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体越来越多地依赖可编辑外部框架（提示、技能、记忆、工具）来指导任务执行，但模型的基础任务解决能力与其框架自我演化能力之间的关系尚不明确：哪些模型能产生有用的框架更新？哪些模型能真正从中受益？

Method: 分析了两种框架自我演化能力：（1）框架更新能力——从执行证据中产生有用持久框架更新的能力；（2）框架受益能力——在任务求解中从更新框架中获益的能力。通过不同能力层级的模型进行对比实验。

Result: （1）框架更新能力与模型基础能力呈扁平关系：不同能力层级模型产生的框架更新带来的增益惊人相似，Qwen3.5-9B的更新增益与Claude Opus~4.6相当；（2）框架受益能力与基础能力呈非单调关系：弱模型受益极少，中等模型受益最大，强模型受益反而低于中等模型。弱模型低收益源于两种失败模式：无法激活相关框架组件，或激活后无法忠实遵循。

Conclusion: 建议将能力预算投入任务求解智能体而非演化器，并在智能体训练中针对框架调用和长程指令遵循进行优化。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed as systems built around editable external harnesses, including prompts, skills, memories and tools, that shape task execution without changing model parameters. Harness self-evolution adapts such agents by updating these harnesses from execution evidence. Yet it remains unclear whether a model's base capability in task-solving predicts its capabilities in harness self-evolution: which models produce useful harness updates, and which actually benefit from them? We analyze two harness self-evolution capabilities: (i) harness-updating, the capability to produce useful persistent harness updates from execution evidence; (ii) harness-benefit, the capability to benefit from updated harnesses during task solving. Our analysis reveals two findings. First, harness-updating is flat in base capability: models from different capability tiers produce harness updates that lead to surprisingly similar gains; even Qwen3.5-9B's updates yield gains comparable to those of Claude Opus~4.6. Second, harness-benefit is non-monotonic in base capability: weak-tier models benefit little from updated harnesses, mid-tier models benefit most, and strong-tier models benefit less than mid-tier. We trace low gains at the weak tier to two failure modes: weak-tier models may fail to activate relevant harness artifacts, or activate them but fail to follow them faithfully. These findings suggest investing capability budget in the task-solving agent rather than the evolver, and targeting harness invocation and long-horizon instruction following in agent training. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/A-EVO-Lab/a-evolve/tree/release/harness-evolution.

</details>


### [134] [EHRBench: An Automated and Reliable EHR-based Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30637)
*Yuzhang Xie,Keqi Han,Yunpeng Xiao,Hejie Cui,Guanchen Wu,Ziyang Zhang,Kai Shu,Jiaying Lu,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EHRBench是一个基于真实EHR数据、通过EHR-LLM-KB自动化流程构建的近百万规模医学决策基准，用于评估LLM在诊断、治疗和预后三大临床决策任务上的表现，结果显示不同LLM存在一致的能力差异和临床可靠性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实临床决策需要LLM支持，但现有基准难以兼顾规模与质量，缺乏基于真实患者EHR数据、能评估实质性生物医学知识和临床推理能力的可靠基准。

Method: 通过EHR-LLM-KB交互流水线：专用LLM自动将EHR轨迹转为结构化模板并实例化为QA条目，同时用知识库系统性地验证和过滤幻觉或模糊关系，构建了涵盖诊断、治疗、预后三类任务的近百万QA条目。

Result: 构建了960,067个QA条目，对30余个代表性LLM进行基准测试，发现不同设置下模型能力趋势一致，验证了EHRBench的可靠性，并揭示了LLM在临床可靠性方面存在的可改进差距。

Conclusion: EHRBench能够大规模、可靠地评估LLM在临床决策任务上的表现，揭示了当前LLM在临床可靠性方面的不足，为构建更可靠的临床LLM系统指明了方向。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making (CDM) is central to real-world clinical workflows, where clinicians infer diagnoses, select treatments, or anticipate future health outcomes under incomplete evidence. LLMs are increasingly used to support these decisions due to strong language capabilities, broad biomedical knowledge, and efficiency, yet the reliability of LLMs on real-world clinical decision tasks remains insufficiently understood. To evaluate CDM models, especially LLM-based models, an ideal and practical medical decision benchmark should be constructed via an automated yet reliable pipeline to ensure both scale and quality. Moreover, the grounding of a CDM benchmark in real patient EHRs can better support evaluation on practical CDM tasks that require substantive biomedical knowledge and clinical inference. To fill the gaps, we introduce EHRBench, an automated and reliable EHR-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLM-based clinical decision-making at scale. To ensure scalability and reliability, EHRBench is constructed through an EHR-LLM-KB(knowledge-base) interaction pipeline. For efficiency, we use a specialized LLM to automatically convert encounter-level EHR trajectories into structured templates and deterministically instantiate the templates into QA items. In parallel, we apply systematic KB-based verification and enrichment to filter hallucinated or ambiguous relations and to improve reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct nearly 1M (960,067) QA items spanning three core inference-required clinical decision tasks: diagnosis, treatment, and prognosis. We benchmark more than 30 representative LLMs on EHRBench and provide detailed analyses of performance and robustness. The results show consistent capability trends across settings, further validating the reliability of EHRBench and highlighting actionable gaps toward clinically reliable LLM systems.

</details>


### [135] [Structure-Induced Information for Rerooting Levin Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30664)
*Jake Tuero,Michael Buro,Laurent Orseau,Levi H. S. Lelis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过学习"rerooter"来隐式分解软子任务，替代传统显式子目标生成，从而提升基于子目标的策略树搜索的可扩展性。设计了三种rerooter（基于聚类、基于启发式、混合式），在复杂环境中实现了更优的在线训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于子目标的策略树搜索需要显式生成子目标，计算开销大且限制了可扩展性，亟需一种更高效的隐式子目标分解方法。

Method: 基于√LTS算法框架，提出三种rerooter设计：(i) 聚类式——利用全局状态空间结构；(ii) 启发式——利用学习到的代价估计；(iii) 混合式——融合两种信号。通过rerooter隐式分解软子任务，避免显式子目标重建与推理。

Result: 所提方法在复杂环境中超越了传统基于子目标的策略树搜索，在测试领域达到了最先进的在线训练效率。

Conclusion: 基于学习到的rerooter隐式分解软子任务是提升策略树搜索可扩展性和效率的有效途径，克服了显式子目标生成带来的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Subgoal-based policy tree search, which uses a policy to guide search, is effective for complex single-agent deterministic problems but often relies on explicit subgoal generation that can incur substantial overhead and hinders scalability. In this paper, we overcome these limitations by using a learned ``rerooter'' through the recently-introduced $\sqrt{\text{LTS}}$ algorithm. A rerooter implicitly decomposes the problem into soft subtasks. While previous work focused on the formal guarantees for given or handcrafted rerooters, in this work we propose three rerooter designs: (i) a clustering-based rerooter that exploits global state-space structure, (ii) a heuristic-based rerooter that leverages learned cost-to-go estimates, and (iii) a hybrid that combines both signals. Our framework avoids having to explicitly reconstruct and reason over generated subgoals, thereby enabling scalable allocation of search effort with significantly lower computational overhead. Empirically, our rerooting-based methods scale to complex environments where subgoal-based policy tree search fails, and achieve state-of-the-art online training efficiency on the domains tested.

</details>


### [136] [Healthcare Mechanisms from Policy-as-Code Search under Strategic Provider Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30680)
*Zihan Wang,Xiang Xu,Hongyuan Zha,Wenhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Medi-Sim多智能体模拟器，将医疗机制设计转化为语言模型的程序合成问题，通过激励扫描复现经典卫生经济学现象，并利用LLM引导的进化代码搜索合成可检查的混合目标程序，在消除编码膨胀的同时将拒绝率减半并保留大部分利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准固定了医疗服务提供者的响应行为，无法评估机制在均衡状态下产生的实际效果，需要一种能捕捉策略性提供者响应行为的机制评估方法。

Method: 将医院机制设计重述为语言模型的程序合成问题，通过Medi-Sim多智能体模拟器（包含编码、选择、延迟、努力、分诊五个策略通道）执行类型化、可检查的规则程序，结合LLM引导的进化代码搜索在规则程序空间中合成最优机制。

Result: 激励扫描复现了经典卫生经济学发现（编码膨胀、利润压力下的低复杂度患者选择、古德哈特漂移）；单一审计通道揭示了压力迁移现象（关闭编码通道使低复杂度选择翻倍以上）；LLM进化搜索合成了可检查的混合目标程序，消除编码膨胀、拒绝率减半、保留大部分利润。

Conclusion: 将医疗机制设计形式化为可搜索的程序空间是有效的，能够发现比现有基准更优的机制，且合成的程序具有可检查性，为实际医疗政策设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Healthcare mechanisms are inseparable from the strategic provider response they induce: existing healthcare AI benchmarks hold this response fixed and so cannot evaluate mechanisms by the equilibrium they produce. We recast hospital mechanism design as program synthesis for language models: typed, inspectable rule programs are executed and scored by Medi-Sim, a multi-agent simulator with five strategic provider channels (coding, selection, delay, effort, triage). An incentive sweep recovers classical health-economics findings as adjacent regimes -- up-coding and low-complexity-patient selection under profit pressure, and Goodhart-style drift where measured performance becomes anti-correlated with true outcomes -- and a single audit lever exposes pressure migration: closing the coding channel more than doubles low-complexity selection. LLM-guided evolutionary code search over the same rule-program space then synthesizes an inspectable mixed-objective program that eliminates up-coding, halves rejection, and retains most of the profit-oriented baseline's funds.

</details>


### [137] [MAVEN: Improving Generalization in Agentic Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30738)
*Omkar Ghugarkar,Vishvesh Bhat,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Asad Aali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAVEN是一个轻量级符号推理框架，通过结构化分解、自适应工具编排和中间验证，将GPT-OSS-120b在MAVEN-Bench上的准确率从48%提升至71%，无需额外训练，且成本仅为前沿闭源模型的1/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在工具调用场景中虽在单个基准上表现良好，但其组合推理策略、保持中间状态及跨域协调工具的能力尚未得到充分探索，缺乏对推理过程本身质量的评估体系。

Method: 提出MAVEN（Modular Agentic Verification and Execution Network），一种轻量级符号推理框架，核心包括结构化分解、自适应工具编排和中间验证三个模块。

Result: 在BFCL v3、TauBench、Tau2Bench、AceBench及自建MAVEN-Bench上评估。MAVEN-Bench显示部分推理质量与端到端成功率之间存在显著差距；MAVEN将GPT-OSS-120b准确率从48%提升至71%，且以约1/10的成本达到与前沿闭源模型竞争的水平。

Conclusion: 轻量级以验证为中心的符号推理框架能够有效增强组合推理能力，推动智能体系统在现实场景中采用更注重过程质量的评估方式。

Abstract: Generalization across agentic tool-calling environments remains a central challenge for reliable agentic reasoning systems. Although large language models achieve strong results on individual benchmarks, their ability to compose reasoning strategies, preserve intermediate states, and coordinate tools across domains remains underexplored. We present MAVEN (Modular Agentic Verification and Execution Network), a lightweight symbolic reasoning scaffold for structured decomposition, adaptive tool orchestration, and intermediate verification. We evaluate MAVEN across established tool-calling benchmarks, including BFCL v3, TauBench, Tau2Bench, AceBench, and introduce MAVEN-Bench, a stress-test benchmark for multi-step mathematical and physical reasoning with explicit verification and adversarial task composition. MAVEN-Bench exposes a substantial gap between partial reasoning quality and end-to-end task success; in direct MAVEN-Bench runs, MAVEN improves its GPT-OSS-120b base model from 48% to 71% accuracy without additional training. It also remains competitive with frontier proprietary baselines while using an open-weight backbone with an estimated cost ratio of roughly 1/10, suggesting that lightweight verification-centered scaffolds can strengthen compositional reasoning and motivate more process-aware evaluation of agents in the wild.

</details>


### [138] [Generating Graph-like Rules for Knowledge Graph Reasoning via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30747)
*Haoxiang Cheng,Yunfei Wang,Chao Chen,Kewei Cheng,Zhipeng Lin,Haoxuan Li,Changjun Fan,Shixuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRiD是一个将图状规则发现重构为离散生成过程的框架，通过监督预训练和强化学习微调的两阶段训练策略，有效挖掘知识图谱中的图状规则（如环路和分支），在六个基准数据集上取得了竞争性的知识图谱补全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规则挖掘方法主要关注链式规则，忽略了图状结构（如环路、分支）中蕴含的丰富关系信息；同时，搜索空间的组合爆炸导致计算瓶颈，而扩散模型等生成方法因其训练目标与规则质量不对齐、非可微评价指标无法直接优化模型，难以直接应用于规则挖掘。

Method: GRiD将图状规则发现重构为以目标关系为条件的离散生成过程，采用两阶段训练策略：1）监督预训练阶段，从KG元图采样子图以捕获结构先验；2）强化学习微调阶段，通过策略梯度优化直接由非可微规则质量指标引导模型优化。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的知识图谱补全任务中取得竞争性性能；消融实验验证了GRiD的效率和鲁棒性，并表明图状规则与链式规则在知识图谱补全中具有互补性。

Conclusion: GRiD通过将规则挖掘转化为条件离散生成过程，有效克服了图状规则搜索的组合爆炸难题，且图状规则能够与链式规则互补提升知识图谱补全效果。

Abstract: Logical rules constitute a cornerstone of knowledge graph (KG) reasoning, valued for their interpretability and ability to model relational patterns. However, existing rule mining methods predominantly focus on simple chain-like rules and therefore neglect the richer relational information encoded in graph-like structures, such as cycles and branches. This limitation is further exacerbated by computational bottlenecks caused by the combinatorial explosion of the search space, which is especially challenging for graph-like rules. Meanwhile, generative approaches such as diffusion models, despite their success in other domains, can not be directly applied to rule mining because their training objectives are not aligned with the goal of learning high-quality rules, and non-differentiable KG rule quality metrics cannot directly guide model optimization. To address these limitations, we propose GRiD, a framework that reformulates graph-like rule discovery as a discrete generative process conditioned on the target relation. GRiD employs a two-phase training strategy. First, supervised pre-training enables GRiD to capture structural priors from subgraphs sampled from the KG meta-graph. Subsequently, reinforcement learning is applied to fine-tune GRiD through policy gradient optimization guided directly by non-differentiable rule-quality metrics. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that GRiD achieves competitive performance on KG completion tasks. Ablation studies confirm the efficiency and robustness of GRiD and further show that graph-like rules complement chain-like rules in KG completion. Our codes and datasets are available in https://github.com/Haoxiang-Cheng/GRiD

</details>


### [139] [PReMISE: Policy Rubrics as Measurement Specifications for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30803)
*Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Tharindu Kumarage,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Anna Rumshisky,Pradeep Natarajan,Venkatesh Saligrama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PReMISE是一个框架，能从人类偏好数据中发现并审计用于LLM评估的rubric集合，通过偏好排序选择和可靠性约束精炼两种修复操作，在保持评估可靠性的同时显著提升准确率并降低可被利用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判的评分高度依赖其所使用的rubric（评估准则），模糊的rubric（如"有用且符合事实"）会奖励那些编造事实或违背用户意图的精美回答。现有rubric来源在可靠性、偏好预测性和对抗鲁棒性上无法同时满足要求，且高评分者间一致性并不意味着低可被利用性。

Method: 提出PReMISE框架，包含两个核心部分：(i) 从成对人类偏好数据中发现策略级rubric集合；(ii) 沿结构充分性、可靠性、偏好拟合和对抗鲁棒性四个维度审计任何rubric集合。同时提出两种修复操作：偏好排名选择（提升配对响应判断准确率）和可靠性约束精炼（降低可利用响应获得高分比率）。

Result: 偏好排名选择将LLM裁判在配对响应上的准确率从65.0%提升至68.6%，与最强基线竞争且在三名裁判中的两名上领先；可靠性约束精炼将利用性响应获得高分的比率从46.4%降至36.0%，而评分者间一致性变化极小（α=0.531→0.519）。

Conclusion: 没有单一原始rubric来源能同时满足可靠、偏好预测和对抗鲁棒；高评分者一致性不意味着低可利用性；PReMISE是唯一在适用性、特异性和有效维度三个指标上同时取得非平凡得分的rubric来源。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly used to evaluate open-ended responses, but their scores depend strongly on the rubrics that condition them. A vague rubric asking for a response to be ``helpful and factual'' can reward polished answers that invent facts or violate user intent. We treat reusable rubrics as measurement specifications: changing the rubric changes the response quality measurement induced by a fixed judge. We introduce PReMISE, a framework that, given pairwise human-preference data, (i) discovers a policy-level rubric set, and (ii) audits any rubric set under LLM-judge use along four axes: structural adequacy, reliability, preference fit, and adversarial robustness. Across rubric sources no raw source is simultaneously reliable, preference-predictive, and adversarially robust; and high inter-rater agreement does not imply low exploitability. PReMISE is the only rubric source to score non-trivially on applicability, specificity, and effective dimensionality simultaneously. We contribute two audit-targeted repair operations: preference-rank selection raises judge accuracy on paired responses from $65.0\%$ to $68.6\%$, competitive with the strongest rubric-discovery baselines and leading on two of three judges in our cross-judge sweep; reliability-constrained refinement reduces the rate at which exploit responses receive high scores from $46.4\%$ to $36.0\%$ with little change in inter-judge agreement ($α{=}.531\to.519$).

</details>


### [140] [SLAT: Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming for Efficient CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30832)
*Jian Yao,Xiongcai Luo,Ran Cheng,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SLAT框架，通过段级自适应修剪策略，在保持推理准确性的同时将推理长度减少50%，建立了更优的准确率-效率帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过RL生成的思维链存在结构性冗余（过度思考），导致计算开销高但不提升答案正确性。现有基于token长度惩罚的方法过于粗糙，无法区分有用推理与冗余内容。

Method: 提出SLAT（Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming）框架：首先理论刻画了正确性-长度权衡下的段次优性，然后基于该准则选择性抑制高概率但低边际效用的冗余段，实现段级自适应修剪。

Result: 在标准基准上，SLAT将推理长度减少50%，同时保持有竞争力的准确率，建立了更优的准确率-效率帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 理论驱动的段级感知修剪是大语言模型高效思维链推理的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models have significantly improved chain-of-thought (CoT) capabilities via reinforcement learning (RL). However, generated reasoning chains frequently suffer from structural redundancy (i.e., \emph{overthinking}), incurring high computational overhead without improving answer correctness. Existing mitigation strategies typically rely on token-uniform length penalties, which provide coarse, segment-agnostic pressure toward shorter outputs and can inadvertently suppress useful reasoning alongside redundancy. To address this, we demonstrate that inefficiency concentrates in high-probability segments with low marginal utility. We derive a theoretical characterization of segment suboptimality under the correctness-length trade-off objective and propose \textsc{SLAT} (Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming), an RL framework that selectively suppresses redundant segments based on this criterion. Empirical results on standard benchmarks indicate that \textsc{SLAT} establishes a superior accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier, reducing reasoning length by $50\%$ relative to uncompressed baselines while maintaining competitive accuracy. Overall, our results suggest that theoretically grounded, segment-aware trimming is a promising direction for efficient CoT reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [141] [Distilling LLM Feedback for Lean Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30861)
